Whole-pipeline measurement of the three native calculation paths (GV-005 "Measure the complete required pipeline"; GV-006 "Correctness precedes performance acceptance"; GV-007 "Limit performance claims to measured evidence"; GV-001 for the attestation runs; Task 7)
Bend version: bend 2.0.39 (driver sha256 2ec160fa4486d33aff51e9eedcc294d6b531dc10c279c00b0c00685dae2045cd)
Compiler: clang version 23.1.1 at /usr/bin/clang (GPU build: emitted C with -DBEND_CUDA=1 at -O3, per verification/gpu-toolchain.txt); NVRTC 13.4.92 compiles each device program; compute-sanitizer 2026.3.0.0 is the launch observer of the attestation runs
Host: Linux 7.2.9-1-MANJARO x86_64, 32 online CPUs. 1-minute load average 15.95 to 23.43 at the series, compile and forged-run `uptime` samples, 16.67 to 71.60 in the attestation records' load_avg lines, and 48 to 57 in activity-log heartbeats taken during the solver 16384 series across every uptime sample below; every series exceeded 16 (half the CPUs) and is labelled "taken on a loaded host; not representative". The display and other graphics and compute clients share the GPU. No number in this file is a performance claim.
GPU: NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090, GPU-0188dad3-4d11-4254-3a0d-50e3808bf464, bus 00000000:0C:00.0, compute capability 8.9 (cubins sm=89), driver 615.71.09, CUDA UMD 13.4
Date: 2026-11-05 06:29 to 07:36 UTC (2026-11-05 00:29 to 01:36 CST); the first implementer's uncommitted edits were reviewed from 06:28 UTC
Environment: export PATH="$HOME/.local/share/writ-toolchains/bend-2.0.39/bend/bin:/opt/cuda/bin:$PATH"; export BEND_NO_TELEMETRY=1; export CUDA_HOME=/opt/cuda. CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES unset and BENDRTS unset in every run (the drivers unset both; every attestation record states it). Commands run from the worktree root unless a path is absolute. The probes were uncommitted at capture (each attestation record's lane_commit is 880c488 marked "uncommitted: M scripts/gpu-validation/..."); their sha256 in every record equals the committed files: Solver.bend 50bc4d0d1da213216559f8fde9f2ea8c2c323f424c0673f10f16e0c59cb3fbcd, Labels.bend 2e8e114da808ff70b34aa2499e750297637349864a6f06bfb08f25eba384c6b6, Transfers.bend d12620da19b521dc8ad7e86857f5b2d95a743ec34afa3b90067515782e291e7e.
Execution: device runs, not individually observed: every timing run with `+RTS --gpu on -RTS` (the plain runs whose times are reported). Device runs, observed: the observed (compute-sanitizer) pass of each `--gpu on` attestation (observe.sh), on the same binary and arguments as the timing runs it sits beside. CPU runs: every timing run with `+RTS --gpu off -RTS` (the same binary; its bang runs on the CPU pool) and the observed pass of each `--gpu off` attestation (0 launches); the CPU reference inside every run (Core.solve_cpu, Dlm.batch, Transfer.batch without `!`); every verifier and check (Core.verify, Dlm.verify_batch, Transfer.admit, the expected-pattern checks), which are CPU code by design; and the forged `labels bench-forged` run, which dispatches nothing to the device.

Probes: scripts/gpu-validation/Solver.bend, Labels.bend and Transfers.bend gain mode `bench SIZE REPS` (SIZE leaves rounded to a power of two, as in run mode; REPS 1..100000). Run, parity, inject and bench-forged modes are unchanged: the diff against 880c488 adds the bench section, one usage line and one `main` arm per probe, and extends each header comment. One GpuLibrary entry and one bang site per binary (BANGS 1 each, below). No library module changed. All inputs are SYNTHETIC and are marked so in the output.

Stage timer. IO.now() returns `io_tick() / 1000000` (Base effs/now.c, sha256 fc4c1d87a4fb2ace7d5b6c57b434cc3d953a15bdf213020912df6c17aa2352d6, quoted below), and io_tick() is clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC) in nanoseconds, so every mark is integer milliseconds of CLOCK_MONOTONIC, truncated: a component below 1 ms prints 0, and a difference of two marks has an error of up to 1 ms. Per repetition the probe takes five marks, each its own IO step:
  t1 before input construction (repetition 1 builds the graph, query tree or request tree; repetitions 2..REPS reuse the resident inputs, so their prep is only the step between marks);
  t2 before the bang;
  t3 after the GpuLibrary call returns;
  t4 after the independent check on the ORIGINAL inputs (Core.verify on the original graph and Scenario stamp; Dlm.verify_batch on the original queries and snapshot; Transfer.admit per original plan against its Context, leaf by leaf);
  t5 after the expected-outcome check (the end of the repetition).
It prints to stderr `bench PROBE rep N prep_ms (t2-t1) device_call_ms (t3-t2) verify_ms (t4-t3) check_ms (t5-t4) total_ms (t5-t1) marks t1..t5`. After the last repetition it takes two more marks around the CPU reference (`after cpu_reference_ms`, not a pipeline stage), then prints run mode's checks over every repetition's result (typed equality with the CPU reference, the expected outcome, the independent check) and two lines summarising the in-repetition checks. Any FAIL exits 1. stdout carries no timing, so the `--gpu off` stdout of the same arguments is byte-comparable.
The harness adds two CLOCK_MONOTONIC readings of its own (trun.py, quoted below, taken immediately before the spawn and after the exit), giving:
  process_wall_ms = exit - spawn: the complete process, every stage included;
  startup_ms = t1(rep 1) - spawn: a bundled component holding exec, runtime initialisation, the CUDA context (cuInit, device and context creation) and the module load of the .gpu with `--gpu on`, and the two stdout header lines; with a stale .gpu it also holds the NVRTC compile;
  tail_ms = exit - t5(rep REPS): the CPU reference, run mode's checks over all REPS results, printing, and process exit (not a pipeline stage; reported so the totals cover the whole process).
So process_wall_ms = startup_ms + sum over repetitions of total_ms + the inter-repetition print steps + tail_ms, to within the 1 ms mark truncation.

Conditions:
  cold: repetition 1 of a fresh process, with startup_ms in front of it. "cold pipeline_ms" = startup_ms + rep-1 total_ms: everything from the spawn to the end of the first verified result. The CUDA context and module load happen before main runs (Task 2 control (e): a run that reaches no bang still opens the context), so they fall in startup_ms, not in rep 1's device_call_ms.
  warm: repetitions 2..20 of the same process, on the same resident inputs.
  compile, not pipeline: one run per probe with its .gpu deleted first (the runtime prints "compiling the GPU program ... is missing or stale"), against a fresh-.gpu run directly after. The regenerated .gpu was cmp-identical to the build's in all three cases, and the original was copied back before the series.

Stage split (missing field). The device_call component is one bundled number: allocation, managed-memory movement, dispatch, every sweep or batch round, and result return as far as the call boundary. Its split into memory movement, dispatch and per-round kernel time is a MISSING FIELD: Nsight Systems (`nsys`) and Nsight Compute (`ncu`) are not installed (`which nsys ncu` finds neither), the Bend runtime exposes no device timer, and managed memory moves by page fault. The tool that would supply it is Nsight Systems (CUDA API, kernel and unified-memory trace); Nsight Compute would supply per-kernel time. No value is estimated. Finding: device_call_ms may not hold the whole bundle. Under `--gpu on` the verify component, which is CPU code, takes far longer than the same component under `--gpu off` on the same binary and inputs (for example labels 16384 warm verify median 149 ms on against 2 ms off; transfers 16384 316 against 26), and the tail grows the same way. The likely cause is that host reads of the result and inputs after the bang fault managed pages back to the host, but this probe cannot establish that: the extra time is reported inside verify_ms and tail_ms as measured, it is not moved into device_call_ms, and no share of it is estimated. Kernel-only numbers do not exist here; any component quoted alone below is labelled a component.

Series method (`bend guide shaders`, "Measuring"): per probe and size, `uptime`, then eight plain runs of `bench SIZE 20` in the order on, off, off, on, on, off, on, on (three interleaved on/off pairs in both orders, on/off, off/on, on/off, then one on/on A-versus-A pair), then `uptime`. Runs are back to back, one at a time; no other run of this lane was in flight. Sizes 16, 1024 and 16384 leaves, REPS 20. Each timing run is a plain run (no observer; the sanitizer changes wall time). Beside each series sit two attestation runs through observe.sh with the same binary and arguments, `--gpu on` and `--gpu off`; observe.sh's own plain pass gives compute_app_seen, the samplers and a second plain wall time, and its observed pass gives the bend_dev launch count. One `--gpu on` attestation covers the five `--gpu on` timing runs of its series (same binary sha256, same arguments, same environment); the timing runs themselves are not individually observed.

Acceptance rule (applied by summarize.py, quoted below; bookkeeping only, every comparison of results is the probe's): a timing run is accepted only if it exited 0, its stdout ends `result: all checks PASS` with no FAIL line, it printed 20 repetition lines, and its attestation for the same mode passed: for `--gpu on` the attestation's gate is PASS; for `--gpu off` both attestation passes print correctness with FAIL 0 (an off run cannot pass the gate by design, and its sanitizer pass exits 255 with "no CUDA API call was instrumented", as in Task 6). Everything else is listed under Rejected with its reason.

## Build
Each probe is built from the worktree root to ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/ (a new directory; earlier binaries and hashes untouched), first to C (BANGS line, emitted_c_sha256), then to the binary, whose build runs the GPU compile and writes NAME.gpu beside it (build.sh, quoted under Harness). Stale *-check.c files left in that directory by the stopped first attempt (05:10 UTC) were deleted before any run; they were never built into a binary.

// IO
// ==

Term io_now_run(Env e, Term* f, IoWork* w) {
  return (Term)(io_tick() / 1000000);
}

static void __attribute__((constructor)) io_now_use(void) {
  io_eff(CID(IO.now), io_now_run);
}
$ strings $(which bend) | grep -A3 'static u64 io_tick'
static u64 io_tick(void) {
  struct timespec ts;
  clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &ts);
  return ts.tv_sec * 1000000000ull + ts.tv_nsec;

### Harness (outside the source tree; quoted so the runs are reproducible; no Writ decision is made by any of them)

--- ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/build.sh ---
#!/bin/sh
# Task 7 build: emitted C (for BANGS) then the native binary, per probe.
export PATH="$HOME/.local/share/writ-toolchains/bend-2.0.39/bend/bin:/opt/cuda/bin:$PATH"; export BEND_NO_TELEMETRY=1; export CUDA_HOME=/opt/cuda
cd /mnt/MediaVolume/Media/src/github.com/spaceghost/writ-lanes/physical-gpu || exit 1
B=../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7
for p in Solver Labels Transfers; do
  n=$(echo $p | tr A-Z a-z)
  s=$(date +%s.%N); timeout 600 bend scripts/gpu-validation/$p.bend -o $B/$n.c > $B/$n.c.log 2>&1; e=$?
  echo "$n.c exit $e secs $(echo "$(date +%s.%N) - $s" | bc)"
  rm -f $B/$n $B/$n.gpu
  s=$(date +%s.%N); timeout 600 bend scripts/gpu-validation/$p.bend -o $B/$n > $B/$n.log 2>&1; e=$?
  echo "$n exit $e secs $(echo "$(date +%s.%N) - $s" | bc)"
done
echo BUILD-DONE
--- end build.sh ---

--- ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/trun.py ---
# Task 7 plain timing launcher (harness only; no Writ decision).
# usage: python3 -I trun.py PREFIX BIN MODE ARGS...
# Runs `timeout 600 BIN +RTS --gpu MODE -RTS ARGS` with stdout to PREFIX.stdout
# and stderr to PREFIX.stderr, and writes PREFIX.time with the CLOCK_MONOTONIC
# milliseconds (the clock IO.now reads) taken just before the spawn and just
# after the exit, the process wall time, the exit status, and the argv.
import subprocess, sys, time
prefix, binp, mode, *args = sys.argv[1:]
argv = ["timeout", "600", binp, "+RTS", "--gpu", mode, "-RTS", *args]
with open(prefix + ".stdout", "wb") as o, open(prefix + ".stderr", "wb") as e:
    t0 = time.clock_gettime_ns(time.CLOCK_MONOTONIC)
    p = subprocess.run(argv, stdout=o, stderr=e)
    t1 = time.clock_gettime_ns(time.CLOCK_MONOTONIC)
with open(prefix + ".time", "w") as f:
    f.write("argv: %s\n" % " ".join(argv))
    f.write("spawn_mono_ms: %.3f\nexit_mono_ms: %.3f\nprocess_wall_ms: %.3f\nexit_status: %d\n"
            % (t0 / 1e6, t1 / 1e6, (t1 - t0) / 1e6, p.returncode))
--- end trun.py ---

--- ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/series.sh ---
#!/bin/sh
# Task 7 series driver (harness only; no Writ decision). Usage: sh series.sh PLAN
# PLAN is one of: small (sizes 16 and 1024, all probes; labels and transfers
# 16384; the compile runs; the forged run) or solver16384.
export PATH="$HOME/.local/share/writ-toolchains/bend-2.0.39/bend/bin:/opt/cuda/bin:$PATH"; export BEND_NO_TELEMETRY=1; export CUDA_HOME=/opt/cuda
unset CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES BENDRTS
WT=/mnt/MediaVolume/Media/src/github.com/spaceghost/writ-lanes/physical-gpu
T7=/mnt/MediaVolume/Media/src/github.com/spaceghost/writ-lanes/writ-build/physical-gpu/task7
RUNS=/mnt/MediaVolume/Media/src/github.com/spaceghost/writ-lanes/writ-build/physical-gpu/runs/task7
LOG=$WT/docs/superpowers/records/2026-11-05-physical-gpu/activity.log
REPS=20
hb() { printf '%s\timplementer\t%s\n' "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)" "$*" >> "$LOG"; }
src() {
  case $1 in
    solver) echo "scripts/gpu-validation/Solver.bend Core.bend GpuLibrary.bend Gpu.bend Dlm.bend DlmGpu.bend Transfer.bend TransferGpu.bend examples/Scenario.bend Negotiation.bend Ledger.bend Frontend.bend Backplane.bend Guard.bend" ;;
    labels) echo "scripts/gpu-validation/Labels.bend Dlm.bend GpuLibrary.bend Core.bend Gpu.bend DlmGpu.bend Transfer.bend TransferGpu.bend examples/Scenario.bend Negotiation.bend Ledger.bend Frontend.bend Backplane.bend Guard.bend" ;;
    transfers) echo "scripts/gpu-validation/Transfers.bend Core.bend Transfer.bend GpuLibrary.bend Gpu.bend Dlm.bend DlmGpu.bend TransferGpu.bend examples/Scenario.bend Negotiation.bend Ledger.bend Frontend.bend Backplane.bend Guard.bend DataOS.bend" ;;
  esac
}
trun() { /usr/bin/python3 -I "$T7/trun.py" "$@"; }
# One series: uptime; on/off, off/on, on/off pairs; one on/on A-versus-A pair; uptime.
series() {
  p=$1; s=$2; D=$T7/series/$p-$s; mkdir -p "$D"
  uptime > "$D/uptime.before"
  i=0
  for m in on off off on on off on on; do
    i=$((i + 1)); trun "$D/$(printf %02d $i)-$m" "$T7/$p" "$m" bench "$s" "$REPS"
  done
  uptime > "$D/uptime.after"
  hb "series $p $s done: $(sed 's/.*load average: //' "$D/uptime.before") -> $(sed 's/.*load average: //' "$D/uptime.after"); exits $(grep -h exit_status "$D"/*.time | awk '{print $2}' | tr '\n' ' ')"
}
# Attestation: observe.sh on the same binary and arguments, on and off.
attest() {
  p=$1; s=$2
  for m in on off; do
    R=$RUNS/$p-$s-attest-$m; rm -rf "$R"
    (cd "$WT" && uptime > "$R.uptime.before" 2>/dev/null; GV_SOURCES="$(src $p)" GV_EMITTED_C=../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/$p.c GV_RUN_DIR=../writ-build/physical-gpu/runs/task7/$p-$s-attest-$m \
      timeout 1300 sh scripts/gpu-validation/observe.sh ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/$p "$m" bench "$s" "$REPS" > "$R.out" 2>&1; echo "exit $?" > "$R.status"; uptime > "$R.uptime.after")
    hb "attest $p $s $m: observe.sh $(cat "$R.status"); $(grep -m1 '^gate:' "$R.out" 2>/dev/null)"
  done
}
mkdir -p "$RUNS"
case $1 in
  small)
    for p in solver labels transfers; do
      mkdir -p "$T7/compile"; uptime > "$T7/compile/$p.uptime.before"
      rm -f "$T7/$p.gpu"; trun "$T7/compile/$p-stale" "$T7/$p" on bench 16 "$REPS"
      cmp "$T7/$p.gpu" "$T7/$p.gpu.orig" > "$T7/compile/$p.cmp" 2>&1; echo "cmp exit $?" >> "$T7/compile/$p.cmp"
      cp "$T7/$p.gpu.orig" "$T7/$p.gpu"
      trun "$T7/compile/$p-fresh" "$T7/$p" on bench 16 "$REPS"
      uptime > "$T7/compile/$p.uptime.after"
    done
    hb "compile runs done (stale .gpu then fresh, size 16)"
    for s in 16 1024; do for p in solver labels transfers; do series $p $s; attest $p $s; done; done
    for p in labels transfers; do series $p 16384; attest $p 16384; done
    mkdir -p "$T7/forged"; uptime > "$T7/forged/uptime.before"
    trun "$T7/forged/labels-bench-forged" "$T7/labels" on bench-forged 16384 "$REPS"
    uptime > "$T7/forged/uptime.after"
    hb "forged control rerun (labels bench-forged 16384 20) exit $(grep exit_status "$T7/forged/labels-bench-forged.time")"
    ;;
  solver16384)
    series solver 16384; attest solver 16384 ;;
esac
hb "series plan $1 finished"
echo PLAN-DONE
--- end series.sh ---

--- ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/summarize.py ---
# Task 7 summary (harness bookkeeping only; no Writ decision). python3 -I summarize.py
# Reads series/<probe>-<size>/NN-<mode>.{time,stdout,stderr} and the attestation
# outputs; prints raw lines, acceptance, medians and spread, ratios.
import os, re, statistics as st, sys
T7 = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
RUNS = os.path.join(T7, "..", "runs", "task7")
def med(xs): return st.median(xs) if xs else None
def f(x): return "-" if x is None else ("%.0f" % x if abs(x) >= 10 else "%.1f" % x)
def spread(xs): return "-" if not xs else "%s..%s" % (f(min(xs)), f(max(xs)))
def load(path):
    try: return open(path).read().strip().split("load average: ")[1]
    except Exception: return "?"
def attest(p, s, m):
    try: out = open(os.path.join(RUNS, "%s-%s-attest-%s.out" % (p, s, m))).read()
    except Exception: return None, None, None
    gate = re.findall(r"^gate: (\S+)", out, re.M)
    corr = re.findall(r"^correctness: (.*)$", out, re.M)
    launches = re.findall(r"^bend_dev_launches: (\S+)", out, re.M)
    return (gate[-1] if gate else None), corr, launches
def parse(prefix):
    t = dict(l.split(": ", 1) for l in open(prefix + ".time").read().splitlines())
    reps = []
    for l in open(prefix + ".stderr").read().splitlines():
        m = re.match(r"bench \S+ rep (\d+) prep_ms (\d+) device_call_ms (\d+) verify_ms (\d+) check_ms (\d+) total_ms (\d+) marks (\d+) (\d+) (\d+) (\d+) (\d+)", l)
        if m: reps.append([int(x) for x in m.groups()])
    cpu = re.findall(r"after cpu_reference_ms (\d+)", open(prefix + ".stderr").read())
    out = open(prefix + ".stdout").read()
    return t, reps, (int(cpu[0]) if cpu else None), out
probes = ["solver", "labels", "transfers"]
sizes = sys.argv[1:] or ["16", "1024", "16384"]
rejected = []
for s in sizes:
    for p in probes:
        D = os.path.join(T7, "series", "%s-%s" % (p, s))
        if not os.path.isdir(D): continue
        g_on, c_on, l_on = attest(p, s, "on"); g_off, c_off, l_off = attest(p, s, "off")
        print("\n### %s size %s" % (p, s))
        lb, la = load(os.path.join(D, "uptime.before")), load(os.path.join(D, "uptime.after"))
        loaded = any(float(x) > 16 for x in (lb + ", " + la).split(", ") if x.replace(".", "").isdigit())
        print("load average before: %s; after: %s%s" % (lb, la, "  [taken on a loaded host; not representative]" if loaded else ""))
        print("attestation on: gate %s, launches %s, correctness %s" % (g_on, l_on, c_on))
        print("attestation off: gate %s, launches %s, correctness %s" % (g_off, l_off, c_off))
        acc = {"on": [], "off": []}
        for fn in sorted(os.listdir(D)):
            if not fn.endswith(".time"): continue
            pre = os.path.join(D, fn[:-5]); mode = fn[:-5].split("-")[1]
            t, reps, cpu, out = parse(pre)
            ex = int(t["exit_status"]); allpass = "result: all checks PASS" in out and not re.search(r"^FAIL", out, re.M)
            corr = "PASS %d FAIL %d" % (len(re.findall(r"^PASS", out, re.M)), len(re.findall(r"^FAIL", out, re.M)))
            reason = []
            if ex != 0: reason.append("exit %d" % ex)
            if not allpass: reason.append("correctness not all PASS")
            if len(reps) != 20: reason.append("%d repetition lines" % len(reps))
            if mode == "on" and g_on != "PASS": reason.append("attestation gate %s" % g_on)
            if mode == "off" and not (c_off and all("FAIL 0" in c for c in c_off)): reason.append("attestation correctness not PASS")
            spawn, exitm = float(t["spawn_mono_ms"]), float(t["exit_mono_ms"])
            startup = reps[0][6] - spawn if reps else None
            tail = exitm - reps[-1][10] if reps else None
            print("run %s mode %s exit %d correctness %s process_wall_ms %.1f startup_ms %s tail_ms %s cpu_reference_ms %s %s"
                  % (fn[:-5], mode, ex, corr, float(t["process_wall_ms"]), f(startup), f(tail), cpu,
                     "ACCEPTED" if not reason else "REJECTED (" + "; ".join(reason) + ")"))
            for r in reps:
                print("  rep %d prep %d device_call %d verify %d check %d total %d" % tuple(r[:6]))
            if reason: rejected.append((p, s, fn[:-5], reason)); continue
            acc[mode].append(dict(wall=float(t["process_wall_ms"]), startup=startup, tail=tail, cpu=cpu, reps=reps))
        print("summary (accepted runs only; median and min..max):")
        print("  %-4s %-4s %-24s %s" % ("mode", "n", "component", "median (spread)"))
        res = {}
        for mode in ("on", "off"):
            rs = acc[mode]; n = len(rs)
            if not n: print("  %s: no accepted run" % mode); continue
            rows = [("cold process_wall_ms", [r["wall"] for r in rs]),
                    ("cold startup_ms (bundled)", [r["startup"] for r in rs]),
                    ("cold rep1 prep_ms", [r["reps"][0][1] for r in rs]),
                    ("cold rep1 device_call_ms", [r["reps"][0][2] for r in rs]),
                    ("cold rep1 verify_ms", [r["reps"][0][3] for r in rs]),
                    ("cold rep1 check_ms", [r["reps"][0][4] for r in rs]),
                    ("cold rep1 total_ms", [r["reps"][0][5] for r in rs]),
                    ("cold pipeline_ms", [r["startup"] + r["reps"][0][5] for r in rs]),
                    ("warm prep_ms", [x[1] for r in rs for x in r["reps"][1:]]),
                    ("warm device_call_ms", [x[2] for r in rs for x in r["reps"][1:]]),
                    ("warm verify_ms", [x[3] for r in rs for x in r["reps"][1:]]),
                    ("warm check_ms", [x[4] for r in rs for x in r["reps"][1:]]),
                    ("warm total_ms", [x[5] for r in rs for x in r["reps"][1:]]),
                    ("tail_ms (cpu ref+checks+exit)", [r["tail"] for r in rs])]
            for name, xs in rows:
                print("  %-4s %-4d %-30s %s (%s)" % (mode, n if not name.startswith("warm") else len(xs), name, f(med(xs)), spread(xs)))
                res[(mode, name)] = med(xs)
        if acc["on"] and acc["off"]:
            for name in ("cold pipeline_ms", "warm total_ms", "cold process_wall_ms"):
                a, b = res[("on", name)], res[("off", name)]
                print("  ratio GPU/CPU %s: %s / %s = %s" % (name, f(a), f(b), ("%.2f" % (a / b)) if b else "undefined (CPU median 0 ms at 1 ms resolution)"))
        on = [fn for fn in sorted(os.listdir(D)) if fn.endswith(".time")]
        if len(on) >= 8:
            a, b = parse(os.path.join(D, on[6][:-5])), parse(os.path.join(D, on[7][:-5]))
            wa = med([x[5] for x in a[1][1:]]); wb = med([x[5] for x in b[1][1:]])
            print("  A-versus-A (runs 07-on, 08-on): process_wall %.1f vs %.1f; warm total median %s vs %s"
                  % (float(a[0]["process_wall_ms"]), float(b[0]["process_wall_ms"]), f(wa), f(wb)))
print("\n### rejected runs")
for r in rejected: print("  %s %s %s: %s" % (r[0], r[1], r[2], "; ".join(r[3])))
if not rejected: print("  (none among the series runs)")
--- end summarize.py ---

--- ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/mkruns.sh ---
#!/bin/sh
# Emit the ## Run transcript for gpu-pipeline.txt from the stored run files.
T7=/mnt/MediaVolume/Media/src/github.com/spaceghost/writ-lanes/writ-build/physical-gpu/task7
RUNS=/mnt/MediaVolume/Media/src/github.com/spaceghost/writ-lanes/writ-build/physical-gpu/runs/task7
WB=/mnt/MediaVolume/Media/src/github.com/spaceghost/writ-lanes/writ-build
rel() { echo "$1" | sed "s#$WB#../writ-build#g"; }
src() {
  case $1 in
    solver) echo "scripts/gpu-validation/Solver.bend Core.bend GpuLibrary.bend Gpu.bend Dlm.bend DlmGpu.bend Transfer.bend TransferGpu.bend examples/Scenario.bend Negotiation.bend Ledger.bend Frontend.bend Backplane.bend Guard.bend" ;;
    labels) echo "scripts/gpu-validation/Labels.bend Dlm.bend GpuLibrary.bend Core.bend Gpu.bend DlmGpu.bend Transfer.bend TransferGpu.bend examples/Scenario.bend Negotiation.bend Ledger.bend Frontend.bend Backplane.bend Guard.bend" ;;
    transfers) echo "scripts/gpu-validation/Transfers.bend Core.bend Transfer.bend GpuLibrary.bend Gpu.bend Dlm.bend DlmGpu.bend TransferGpu.bend examples/Scenario.bend Negotiation.bend Ledger.bend Frontend.bend Backplane.bend Guard.bend DataOS.bend" ;;
  esac
}
one() { # prefix label
  pre=$1
  echo "\$ /usr/bin/python3 -I ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/trun.py $(rel "$pre") $(rel "$(sed -n 's/^argv: timeout 600 \([^ ]*\) +RTS --gpu \([a-z]*\) -RTS \(.*\)$/\1 \2 \3/p' "$pre.time")")"
  echo "  argv: $(rel "$(sed -n 's/^argv: //p' "$pre.time")")"
  echo "  [exit status: $(sed -n 's/^exit_status: //p' "$pre.time"); process_wall_ms $(sed -n 's/^process_wall_ms: //p' "$pre.time"); spawn_mono_ms $(sed -n 's/^spawn_mono_ms: //p' "$pre.time"); exit_mono_ms $(sed -n 's/^exit_mono_ms: //p' "$pre.time")]"
  echo "  stdout sha256 $(sha256sum < "$pre.stdout" | cut -c1-64); $(grep -c '^PASS' "$pre.stdout") PASS, $(grep -c '^FAIL' "$pre.stdout") FAIL; last line: $(tail -1 "$pre.stdout")"
  echo "  stderr (raw):"
  sed "s#$WB#../writ-build#g; s/^/    /" "$pre.stderr"
}
echo "### Compile runs (stale .gpu deleted first; labelled \"compile, not pipeline\")"
for p in solver labels transfers; do
  echo; echo "\$ uptime"; sed 's/^/ /' "$T7/compile/$p.uptime.before"
  echo "\$ rm -f ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/$p.gpu"
  one "$T7/compile/$p-stale"
  echo "\$ cmp ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/$p.gpu ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/$p.gpu.orig"
  sed 's/^/  /' "$T7/compile/$p.cmp"
  echo "\$ cp ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/$p.gpu.orig ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/$p.gpu"
  one "$T7/compile/$p-fresh"
  echo "\$ uptime"; sed 's/^/ /' "$T7/compile/$p.uptime.after"
done
for s in 16 1024 16384; do for p in solver labels transfers; do
  D=$T7/series/$p-$s; [ -d "$D" ] || continue
  echo; echo "### Series $p size $s (REPS 20; order on off off on on off on on: pairs on/off, off/on, on/off, then the A-versus-A pair on/on)"
  echo "\$ uptime"; sed 's/^/ /' "$D/uptime.before"
  for f in "$D"/*.time; do one "${f%.time}"; done
  echo "\$ uptime"; sed 's/^/ /' "$D/uptime.after"
  for m in on off; do
    R=$RUNS/$p-$s-attest-$m
    echo; echo "#### Attestation $p size $s --gpu $m (observe.sh: same binary and arguments as the timing runs above)"
    echo "\$ uptime"; sed 's/^/ /' "$R.uptime.before"
    echo "\$ GV_SOURCES=\"$(src $p)\" GV_EMITTED_C=../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/$p.c GV_RUN_DIR=../writ-build/physical-gpu/runs/task7/$p-$s-attest-$m timeout 1300 sh scripts/gpu-validation/observe.sh ../writ-build/physical-gpu/task7/$p $m bench $s 20"
    sed "s#$WB#../writ-build#g" "$R.out"
    echo "[exit status: $(sed 's/exit //' "$R.status") (check-record.sh gate verdict: 0 PASS, 1 FAIL)]"
    echo "\$ uptime"; sed 's/^/ /' "$R.uptime.after"
  done
done; done
echo; echo "### Forged control, rerun on the Task 7 labels binary (no device call; must be rejected)"
echo "\$ uptime"; sed 's/^/ /' "$T7/forged/uptime.before"
one "$T7/forged/labels-bench-forged"
echo "  stdout PASS/FAIL lines:"; grep -E '^(PASS|FAIL|result)' "$T7/forged/labels-bench-forged.stdout" | sed 's/^/    /'
echo "\$ uptime"; sed 's/^/ /' "$T7/forged/uptime.after"
--- end mkruns.sh ---

## Result
Every series below is labelled: taken on a loaded host; not representative. Load is the 1-minute `uptime` average before -> after the series (all three averages are in the transcript). Times are medians in ms with min..max; "cold" n = 5 on-runs or 3 off-runs, "warm" pools repetitions 2..20 of the same runs (95 on, 57 off). Every component is a component; the complete pipelines are "cold pipeline" (spawn to the end of the first verified result) and "warm total" (one whole repetition: prep + device call + verify + check). GPU first in every ratio.

Cold process wall also contains the tail (CPU reference plus run mode's checks over all 20 results; solver 16384 on 112060 (109607..143305), off 45117 (42007..102233)), which is not a pipeline stage; the full per-component medians, including prep, check and tail, are in the Summary above. The A-versus-A pairs differ by up to 23 percent in process wall (solver 16384: 388125 against 477950) and by up to 23 percent in the warm total median (solver 16384: 12329 against 15196), which bounds what any difference below that size means at this load.

Slower cases, as measured: every measured case. In all nine path-and-size cells, under both conditions where a ratio is defined, the complete `--gpu on` pipeline took longer than the `--gpu off` pipeline of the same binary on this host at the stated load. No speedup was measured, so this file contains no speedup sentence.
Break-even, as measured: not reached at any measured size (16, 1024, 16384 leaves) for any path. The smallest measured ratio of the two pipeline columns (cold pipeline, warm total) is the solver's: 2.07 (warm total, size 1024) and 2.35 to 2.60 at 16384. The smallest ratio in the table overall is 1.96 (cold process wall, solver 1024), a column that includes the tail and is not a pipeline column. (Task 9 wording fix; no number changed.) Nothing is stated about sizes that were not measured, and no trend is extrapolated.
Cold start: under `--gpu on` the bundled startup (exec, runtime init, CUDA context, module load) is 123 to 204 ms median against 10 to 39 ms under `--gpu off`, and it dominates the cold pipeline at sizes 16 and 1024 for every path.
Compile, not pipeline: with the .gpu deleted, startup was 1019 ms (solver), 486 ms (labels) and 11541 ms (transfers), against 157, 123 and 154 ms in the fresh-.gpu run straight after (one run each, load 20.30 to 19.11; taken on a loaded host; not representative). The difference holds the runtime's NVRTC compile of the device program and is not part of any pipeline number above. Each regenerated .gpu was cmp-identical to the build's.
Verify under the device: the verify component (CPU code) is much larger under `--gpu on` than under `--gpu off` (labels 16384 warm 149 against 2; transfers 16384 316 against 26; solver 16384 4707 against 1592). This is recorded as measured and not explained; see the Stage split paragraph. It is part of every complete pipeline above, which is why the pipelines, not the device-call component, are compared.

### Rejected
- Task 5 forged run (verification/gpu-injection.txt, `labels bench-forged 16384 200` through observe.sh): plain-pass wall_ms 1164, 0 bend_dev launches, correctness PASS 3 FAIL 3, probe exit 1, gate FAIL. REJECTED: correctness not all PASS, gate FAIL, and no device call. Its time is not accepted and appears in no table or ratio above.
- Task 7 rerun of the same control on this lane's labels binary (`labels +RTS --gpu on -RTS bench-forged 16384 20`, transcript above): exit 1, process wall 443.8 ms, 0 device calls by construction, correctness 3 PASS 3 FAIL (tree equality 0 of 20, expected pattern 0 of 20, Dlm.verify_batch 0 of 20; the digest line PASSES 20 of 20, the structural collision of Task 5, and no digest is equality evidence). REJECTED: exit 1, correctness not all PASS, and no attestation can pass a run with no device call. Its time is not accepted.
- Timing series runs rejected: none. 72 of 72 accepted: 45 `--gpu on` (5 per series), each covered by the gate-PASS attestation of the same binary and arguments beside its series, and 27 `--gpu off` (3 per series), each covered by its series' off attestation with correctness FAIL 0.
- Attestation passes that do not pass the gate, by design: each `--gpu off` attestation (9) has gate FAIL with the reason recorded (requests no device; 0 launches; compute-sanitizer exit 255, no CUDA API call instrumented). They attest the off runs' correctness only, as the acceptance rule says, and none of their times is used.

Requirement mapping. GV-005: every stage of the complete pipeline is in the totals (startup, prep, device call, verify, check, tail), with the bundled ones named (startup, device call). GV-006: only runs whose correctness is all PASS and whose attestation passed are compared; the forged runs are rejected. GV-007: every ratio above carries its size, condition and configuration (bend 2.0.39, driver 615.71.09, RTX 4090 GPU-0188dad3-4d11-4254-3a0d-50e3808bf464, CUDA/NVRTC 13.4.92, the task7 .gpu hashes in Build, 32 threads by default, the stated load), no threshold is set, and nothing is claimed beyond the measured sizes on this configuration. The split of the device call into memory movement, dispatch and kernel time is a missing field (Nsight Systems), so the measurement is incomplete for those components.

## Limits
- Loaded host: the 1-minute load average was above 16 on 32 CPUs at every series boundary but one (15.95, the start of the solver 16384 series), and about 10 to 16 for a few minutes inside that series (Task 9 wording fix; the values are in each series' table and transcript), with the display and other clients on the GPU. Every series is labelled "taken on a loaded host; not representative". The plan allows one repeat when the load drops. The 1-minute load was above 16 at every series boundary except the start of the solver 16384 series (15.95, ending at 23.32). It fell to about 10 to 16 for a few minutes during that series (heartbeats 06:59 to 07:05 UTC), while the lane's own series occupied the host, and it was 23.14 when the last series ended. So no series was repeated, and nothing here is representative of this configuration on a quiet host.
- No timing number in this file is a performance claim. The ratios are of the measured complete pipelines on this host at this load; they set no threshold and say nothing about other sizes, inputs, devices, drivers, Bend versions or a quiet host.
- Missing field: the split of device_call_ms into managed-memory movement, dispatch and per-round kernel time. The tool that would supply it is Nsight Systems (`nsys`; per-kernel time from Nsight Compute, `ncu`); neither is installed, the runtime has no device timer, and no value is estimated. Kernel-only numbers do not exist here.
- The stage boundaries do not isolate memory movement: under `--gpu on`, verify_ms and tail_ms (CPU code) are much larger than under `--gpu off`; the extra is reported where it was measured, unexplained and unattributed (managed-memory faults on host access are the likely but unestablished cause).
- Timer resolution is 1 ms (IO.now truncates CLOCK_MONOTONIC to integer milliseconds). At size 16, and for most `--gpu off` components at 1024 and some at 16384, the per-repetition components are 0 to 2 ms, so their medians and the warm ratios built from them are at the resolution floor; a CPU median of 0 ms makes a ratio undefined, and it is printed as undefined.
- Whether each mark sees fully evaluated values is not established by the probe: the marks are IO steps, and the numbers are consistent with each step's value being computed before the next mark (prep is nonzero only in repetition 1; off-mode verify is nonzero), but the probe does not prove that no part of a stage's work is deferred into a later stage.
- Timing runs are plain and not individually observed; the attestation for each series (same binary, arguments and environment) is one observed run per mode. A timing run's own launch count is not recorded.
- The `--gpu off` CPU match uses the same binary with the bang on the CPU pool, as the brief specifies; it is not a separately optimised CPU implementation.
- Startup and tail are process-level components measured by the harness clock around the spawn; startup bundles exec, runtime init, CUDA context and module load (and, in the compile runs, NVRTC); they are not split further.
- One attestation pair per series; three on/off pairs and one A-versus-A pair per size; sizes 16, 1024, 16384 only; REPS 20 only; one graph shape and one alternating query/request pattern per path (the SYNTHETIC run-mode inputs). The combined three-path binary (GpuExample) and the library corpus were not timed.
- Not exercised: `--gpu <size>`, the default (no +RTS) mode, CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES set, `--threads` other than the runtime default, device memory checking (all kernels excluded from the sanitizer), Nsight, a quiet host.
- The forged run from Task 5 (observe.sh plain-pass wall_ms 1164) is rejected and its time is not accepted; the Task 7 rerun of the same control on this lane's binary is rejected too. No digest is equality evidence (Task 5 ruling): acceptance here rests on the typed in-process comparisons and the independent checks, never on the digest lines.
- `bend Tests.bend` and `bend PROOF.bend` were not run: no library, law or proof source changed.
